                        $('.stock_count_new').text('');

                        $('#instocknotify-container').show();
                        $('.productAttributes-message').show();

    });
});

I owned a pair previously that that I outgrew, so I ordered another. I'm a 10.5 on a Brannock device, D/Medium width, so I ordered size 10.5. I prefer more space than not with my shoes (for my toes), but these were just a tad too much space for me. My previous Indy's were 9.5's. I've returned the 10.5's and will be ordering some 10's just as soon as my return is set. I've had my 9.5 Indy's for over 10 years. I always took good care of them, and so they still actually look good as new, leather is soft as ever (these were the reddish looking brown boots). next_prev();
$('#product-listing-container').on('DOMSubtreeModified',function(){
    next_prev();
});
function next_prev(){
    $('.pagination-item--next').children('a').attr('href',$('.pagination-item.pagination-item--current').next().children('a').attr('href'));
    $('.pagination-item--previous').children('a').attr('href',$('.pagination-item.pagination-item--current').prev().children('a').attr('href'));
}

<script>
 $(document).ready(function() {
    $('ul.faqtabs').each(function(){
      var active, content, links = $(this).find('a');
      active = links.first().addClass('active');
      content = $(active.attr('href'));
      links.not(':first').each(function () {
        $($(this).attr('href')).hide();
      });
      $(this).find('a').click(function(e){
        active.removeClass('active');
        content.hide();
        active = $(this);
        content = $($(this).attr('href'));
        active.addClass('active');
        content.show();
        return false;
      });
    });
  });
</script>

<script>
    $('.productView-thumbnail').on('click',function(){  
            
        if($(this).children('span').text() === 'has video'){
            $('.productView-image iframe').remove();
            $(this).parent().parent().parent().siblings('.productView-image').addClass('has_video');
            $('.productView-image').append($(this).children('iframe').clone());
        }
        else{
            $('.productView-image iframe').remove();
            $(this).parent().parent().parent().siblings('.productView-image').removeClass('has_video');
        }
    });
</script>

function SirvToggleMT(zoom, spin) {

        $('#ImageContainer').hide();

        //$('.productView-image').removeClass('has_video');
    }
    return false;
}
